Integrating by part and using boundary condition (
3.30
), we have

\displaystyle II_{52}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{k}C^{k}_{j}\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{x}^{j-1}\partial_{y}^{4}v^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k-j}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l}dydx

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{k}C^{k}_{j}\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{x}^{j-1}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k-j}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l}dydx

\displaystyle-2l\sum_{j=1}^{k}C^{k}_{j}\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{x}^{j-1}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k-j}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l-1}dydx

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle II_{521}+II_{522}+II_{523}.

Using the Hölder inequality, estimates (
A.6
), (
A.7
)
and (
3.17
), we have

\displaystyle|II_{521}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{4}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}q^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{4}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}q\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+o_{L}(1)\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x))\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*},l,k,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{8}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{8}).

Similarly, using (
A.4
), (
A.6
), (
A.7
)
and (
3.15
), we also have

\displaystyle|II_{522}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{4}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+o_{L}(1)\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k\rangle}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{8}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{8}).

Finally, we use the estimates (
A.4
) and (
A.7
) to obtain

\displaystyle|II_{523}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}q^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle+C_{k}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}q^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]+1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x)^{2}

\displaystyle+o_{L}(1)C_{k}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},l,\delta_{0}}\mathcal{E}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]+1\rangle}_{l}(x))\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{4}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{4}).

Based on the above estimates from terms
II_{521}
to
II_{523}
, we can obtain the estimate

|II_{52}|\leq o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*},l,k,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{8}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{8}),

which, together with estimate (
3.47
), yields the following estimate directly

|II_{5}|\leq o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*},l,k,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{8}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{8}).

Therefore, we complete the proof of estimate (
3.37
).
∎

Proof of estimate (
3.38
)
.

Obviously, we have

\displaystyle II_{6}=

\displaystyle-2\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l}dxdy,

\displaystyle+2\sum_{j=1}^{k}C^{k}_{j}\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{x}^{j-1}\partial_{y}^{2}v^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k-j}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l}dxdy

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle II_{61}+II_{62}.

Let us write

\displaystyle II_{61}=

\displaystyle-2\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l}\chi dxdy

\displaystyle-2\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l}(1-\chi)dxdy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle:II_{611}+II_{612}

Integrating by part and using boundary condition (
3.30
), we have

\displaystyle II_{611}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}|^{2}\partial_{y}\{\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l}\chi\}dxdy

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}|^{2}\{\partial_{y}^{2}u^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l}\chi+2l\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l-1}\chi+\frac{1}{\delta}\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l}\chi^{\prime}\}dxdy.

Then, choosing
\delta=\delta_{0}
and using the estimate (
A.4
), we get

\displaystyle|II_{611}|

\displaystyle\leq C_{l,\delta_{0}}(\|\partial_{y}^{3}u^{\epsilon}\chi\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}u^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},l,\delta_{0}}(\|\partial_{y}^{3}u^{\epsilon}\chi|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}u^{\epsilon}|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}^{4}v^{\epsilon}\chi\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x)^{2}.

Using the Hölder inequality, lower bound assumption (
3.12
)
and divergence-free condition, we have

\displaystyle|II_{612}|

\displaystyle\leq o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},\delta_{0}}\|\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\sqrt{u^{\epsilon}}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},\delta_{0}}(\|\partial_{y}^{2}u^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+o_{L}(1)\|\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x)^{2}.

Then, we have the estimate

|II_{61}|\leq o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{4}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{4}).

(3.48)

Finally, let us deal with term
II_{62}
.
Due to
k\geq 2
, integrating by part and using boundary
condition (
3.30
), we have

\displaystyle II_{62}=

\displaystyle-2\sum_{j=1}^{k}C^{k}_{j}\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{x}^{j-1}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k-j}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l}dxdy

\displaystyle-2\sum_{j=1}^{k}C^{k}_{j}\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{x}^{j-1}\partial_{y}^{2}v^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k-j}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l}dxdy

\displaystyle-4l\sum_{j=1}^{k}C^{k}_{j}\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{x}^{j-1}\partial_{y}^{2}v^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k-j}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l-1}dxdy

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle II_{621}+II_{622}+II_{623}.

Using the Sobolev inequality, estimates
(
A.4
), (
A.8
), (
3.15
) and (
3.17
),
it is easy to deduce

\displaystyle|II_{621}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle+C_{k}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k,l}(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]+1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x)

\displaystyle+o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k,l}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{4}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\times(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]+1\rangle}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})

\displaystyle\leq

Similarly, for the case of
k\geq 2
, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle|II_{623}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}v^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle+C_{k}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}v^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k}(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]+1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x)^{2}

\displaystyle+o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]+1\rangle}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{4}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{4}).

and

\displaystyle|II_{622}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}v^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle+C_{k}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}v^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k}(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x)

\displaystyle+o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]+1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x)

where we have used the following inequality in the last inequality

Thus, we can obtain the following estimate for all

(3.49)

Thus, the combination of estimates (

) and (

) yields directly

|II_{6}|\leq o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{8}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{8}),

Proof of estimate (
3.39
)
.

Let us write

\displaystyle II_{7}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{k}C^{k}_{j}\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{x}^{j}u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k-j}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l}\chi(\frac{y}{\delta_{0}})\langle y\rangle^{2l}dydx

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{k}C^{k}_{j}\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{x}^{j}u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k-j}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l}(1-\chi(\frac{y}{\delta_{0}}))\langle y\rangle^{2l}dydx

\displaystyle II_{71}+II_{72}.

First of all, let deal with the term
II_{71}
.
Using Hölder inequality,
estimates (
A.3
) and (
A.8
), we can obtain

\displaystyle|II_{71}|\leq

\displaystyle|\sum_{j=1}^{k}C^{k}_{j}\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{x}^{j}u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k-j}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l}\chi dydx|

(3.50)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}(\delta_{0}+\epsilon)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}}{u^{\epsilon}}\chi\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\chi\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\sqrt{u^{\epsilon}}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\chi\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle+C_{k}(\delta_{0}+\epsilon)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}}{u^{\epsilon}}\chi\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\chi\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\sqrt{u^{\epsilon}}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\chi\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{L}(1)C_{k,k_{*},k^{*},\delta_{0}}(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}v^{\epsilon}|_{x=0}\|_{H^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]+1\rangle}\partial_{y}v^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}H^{2}_{y}})\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x)

\displaystyle+o_{L}(1)C_{k,k_{*},\delta_{0}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]+1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{4}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{4}).

Using the Hölder inequality, we have

\displaystyle|II_{72}|=

\displaystyle|\sum_{j=1}^{k}C^{k}_{j}\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{x}^{j}u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k-j}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l}(1-\chi)dydx|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\|{\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}}(1-\chi)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}(1-\chi)\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}(1-\chi)\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}.

Away from the boundary

, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}(1-\chi)\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}(1-\chi)\langle y\rangle^{l}|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+o_{L}(1)\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}(1-\chi)\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|II_{72}|\leq

\displaystyle\|{\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}v^{\epsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}H^{1}_{y}}\|\sqrt{u^{\epsilon}}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\times(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}(1-\chi)\langle y\rangle^{l}|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*}}\|\sqrt{u^{\epsilon}}\partial_{x}^{\langle k\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{8}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{8}),

which, together with the estimate (

), yields directly

which is the inequality (

).

Proof of estimate (

.

\displaystyle\leq

Similarly, it is easy to get that

\displaystyle|II_{82}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,l}\|\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

(3.52)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\leq

.
Indeed, Let us write

\displaystyle II_{83}=

\displaystyle-2l\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l-1}dydx

\displaystyle-2l\sum_{j=1}^{k}C^{k}_{j}\int_{0}^{L}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{x}^{j}u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k-j}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{2l-1}dydx

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle II_{831}+II_{832}.

Using the Hölder inequality, we get

|II_{831}|\leq C_{l}\|\sqrt{u^{\epsilon}}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\sqrt{u^{\epsilon}}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}.

Using the equivalent relation (
3.11
)
and lower bound (
3.12
), it holds for all
\delta\leq\delta_{0}

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{u^{\epsilon}}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{u^{\epsilon}}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}(1-\chi)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}+\|\sqrt{u}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}\chi\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{L}{\delta}\|u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}(1-\chi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}+\delta\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}\chi\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*}}(\|u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}+\|\sqrt{u^{\epsilon}}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}),

where we have chosen
\delta=L^{\frac{1}{2}}
and used estimate
(
A.6
) in the last inequality. Thus, it holds

|II_{831}|\leq o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},l}X^{k}_{l}(x)^{2}.

Using Sobolev inequality, estimates (
A.4
),
(
A.8
), (
3.13
), (
3.14
) and (
3.15
), we get

\displaystyle|II_{832}|\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,l}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}v^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle+C_{k,l}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}v^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{y}q\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k,l}(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]+1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x)

\displaystyle+o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k,l}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]+1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{4}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{4}),

where we have used the following inequality in the last inequality

\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}\leq C_{k_{*},k^{*}}(\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{2}q^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l-1}(1-\chi)|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\langle[\frac{k-1}{2}]\rangle}\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}).

Thus, we can obtain the following estimate

|II_{83}|\leq o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{4}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{4}).

This, together with the estimates (
3.51
) and (
3.52
), yields directly

|II_{8}|\leq o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{4}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{4}),

which is the inequality (
3.40
).
∎

Now let us give the proof for the Proposition
3.1
.

Proof of Proposition
3.1
.

Based on the estimates obtained so far, we can complete the proof of
Proposition
3.1
in this subsection.
First of all, we give the proof for the estimate.
For three parameters
R,k_{*}
and
k^{*}
, which will be defined later, we have

\displaystyle L^{\epsilon}_{*}:=

\displaystyle\sup\left\{L\in[0,1]|X^{k}_{l}(x)^{2}+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(x)^{2}\leq R,\quad u^{\epsilon}(x,y)\geq k_{*}(\delta_{0}+\epsilon),\quad(x,y)\in[0,L^{\epsilon}]\times[\delta_{0},+\infty);\right.

\displaystyle\left.k_{*}(y+\epsilon)\leq u^{\epsilon}(x,y)\leq k^{*}(y+\epsilon),\ (x,y)\in[0,L^{\epsilon}]\times[0,\delta_{0}]\right\},

where the constant
\delta_{0}
is defined by the property of initial
data
u_{0}(y)
.
Recall the estimates (
3.14
), (
3.15
), (
3.16
)
and (
3.18
) in Lemma
3.2
, we have

\sum_{j=1}^{3}\|\partial_{x}^{\langle k-1\rangle}\partial_{y}^{j}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}^{4}v^{\epsilon}\chi\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\leq o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},l,k,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{16}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{16}),

and estimate (
3.28
) in Lemma
3.3

\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(x)^{2}\leq\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(0)^{2}+o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{16}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{16}),

then we can obtain the following estimate

X^{k}_{l}(x)^{2}\leq\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(0)^{2}+o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{16}+X^{k}_{l}(x)^{16}).

(3.53)

On the other hand, the quantity
\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(x)
can be controlled as follows

\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(x)\leq\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)+o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*}}X^{k}_{l}(x).

(3.54)

Then, the combination of estimates (
3.53
) and (
3.54
) yields directly

X^{k}_{l}(x)^{2}+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(x)^{2}\leq\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(0)^{2}+C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)^{16})+o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}X^{k}_{l}(x)^{16}.

(3.55)

It is easy to check that there exists a constant
only depends on the initial data
u_{0}
such that

\mathcal{E}^{k}_{l}(0)+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(0)\leq C(u_{0}),

(3.56)

which can be found in (
C.1
) in Appendix
C
.
Then, the combination of (
3.55
) and (
3.56
) yields directly

X^{k}_{l}(x)^{2}+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(x)^{2}\leq C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+C(u_{0}))+o_{L}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}X^{k}_{l}(x)^{16}.

Thus, we concludes for all
L_{1}\leq L^{\epsilon}
that

X^{k}_{l}(L_{1})^{2}+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(L_{1})^{2}\leq C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+C(u_{0}))+o_{L_{1}}(1)C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}R^{16}.

Let us choose constants
k_{*}=\frac{1}{8},~k^{*}=4
and

R=4C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+C(u_{0}))
, then we have

X^{k}_{l}(L_{1})^{2}+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(L_{1})^{2}\leq\overline{C}_{k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+C(u_{0}))+o_{L_{1}}(1)\overline{C}_{k,l,\delta_{0}}\{4(1+C(u_{0}))\}^{16}.

(3.57)

Here we denote
\overline{C}_{k,l,\delta_{0}}:=C_{k_{*},k^{*},k,l,\delta_{0}}|_{(k_{*},k^{*})=(\frac{1}{8},4)}
.
Choose the time
L_{1}
small enough such that

o_{L_{1}}(1)\overline{C}_{k,l,\delta_{0}}\{4(1+C(u_{0}))\}^{16}\leq\overline{C}_{k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+C(u_{0})),

and hence, we deduce from (
3.57
) that

X^{k}_{l}(L_{1})^{2}+\mathcal{B}^{k}_{l}(L_{1})^{2}\leq 2\overline{C}_{k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+C(u_{0}))=\frac{R}{2}.

(3.58)

It is easy to check that

u^{\epsilon}(x,y)=u^{\epsilon}(0,y)+\int_{0}^{x}\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}(s,y)ds,

(3.59)

and

\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}(x,y)=\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}(x,0)+\int_{0}^{y}\partial_{y}\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}(x,\tau)d\tau.

Then, using the boundary condition (
2.2
), we have for all
y\in[0,\delta_{0}]

|\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}(x,y)|\leq y\|\partial_{y}\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}(x,y)\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}([0,y])}\leq y\|\partial_{y}^{2}\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}(x,y)\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{y}([0,+\infty))},

and hence, it is easy to check that

|\int_{0}^{x}\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}(x,y)dx|\leq\sqrt{x}y\|\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

(3.60)

Then, the combination of representation (
3.59
) and estimate (
3.60
) yields directly

u^{\epsilon}(x,y)\geq u^{\epsilon}(0,y)-|\int_{0}^{L}\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}(s,y)ds|\geq\frac{1}{2}(y+\epsilon)-\sqrt{L}y\|\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}.

Choose

L_{2}:=\min\{\frac{1}{32\overline{C}_{k,l,\delta_{0}}\{1+C(u_{0})\}},L_{1}\}
,
then we have

u^{\epsilon}(x,y)\geq\frac{1}{2}(y+\epsilon)-\frac{1}{4}y\geq\frac{1}{4}(y+\epsilon)=2k_{*}(y+\epsilon),\text{~for~all~}(x,y)\in[0,L_{2}]\times[0,\delta_{0}].

Similarly, it is easy to check that

u^{\epsilon}(x,y)\leq u^{\epsilon}(0,y)+|\int_{0}^{L}\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}(s,y)ds|\leq\frac{3}{2}(y+\epsilon)+\sqrt{L}y\|\partial_{y}^{3}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}.

Choose

L_{3}=\min\{L_{2},\frac{1}{8\overline{C}_{k,l,\delta_{0}}\{1+C(u_{0})\}}\}
,
then we have

u^{\epsilon}(x,y)\leq\frac{3}{2}(y+\epsilon)+\frac{1}{2}y=2(y+\epsilon)=\frac{1}{2}k^{*}(y+\epsilon),\text{~for~all~}(x,y)\in[0,L_{3}]\times[0,\delta_{0}].

Finally, using the Sobolev inequality, Hölder inequality
and divergence-free condition, we have

|\int_{0}^{L}\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}(x,y)dx|\leq\int_{0}^{L}\|\partial_{y}\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{y}}dx\leq\sqrt{L}\|\partial_{y}^{2}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}},

which, together with (
3.59
), yields directly

u^{\epsilon}(x,y)\geq u^{\epsilon}(0,y)-|\int_{0}^{L}\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}(s,y)ds|\geq(\delta_{0}+\epsilon)-\sqrt{L}\|\partial_{y}^{2}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}.

Choose

L_{4}:=\min\{L_{3},\frac{\delta_{0}^{2}}{32\overline{C}_{k,l,\delta_{0}}\{1+C(u_{0})\}}\}
,
then we have

u^{\epsilon}(x,y)\geq\frac{1}{2}(\delta_{0}+\epsilon)-\frac{1}{4}\delta_{0}\geq\frac{1}{4}(\delta_{0}+\epsilon)=2k_{*}(\delta_{0}+\epsilon),\text{~for~all~}(x,y)\in[0,L_{4}]\times[\delta_{0},+\infty).

Obviously, we conclude that there exists
L_{4}>0
depending on

k,l,\delta_{0}
and the initial data(hence independent of parameter
\epsilon
)
such that for all
L\leq\min\{L_{4},L^{\epsilon}\}
, the estimates (
3.8
)
and (
3.9
) in Proposition
3.1
hold true.
Of course, it holds that
L_{4}\leq L^{\epsilon}
.
Indeed otherwise, our criterion about the continuation of the solution would
contradict the definition of
L^{\epsilon}_{*}
.
Then, taking
L_{a}=L_{4}
, we obtain the estimates (
3.8
)
and (
3.9
).
Furthermore, due to the equation (
3.2
),
we can apply the estimates (
3.58
) and
(
A.19
)(see Proposition
A.3
in Appendix
A
) to get

\sum_{0\leq 2\alpha+\beta\leq 2k+1}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{\beta}\partial_{y}v^{\epsilon}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\leq C_{k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+C(u_{0})),

where
C(u_{0})
is a constant depends on the initial data
u_{0}
.
Therefore, the proof of Proposition
3.1
is completed.
∎

3.2.  Local existence of Prandtl-Hartmann system

In this subsection, we will give the proof of local existence of
original system (
1.6
).
For any
\epsilon>0
and some large
N>0
, we introduce an artificial truncation at
y=N

and then send
N\rightarrow\infty
.
For any
\epsilon\in(0,1]
and large
N
,
let us consider the problem on
(x,y)\in(0,L)\times(0,N)
,

\left\{\begin{aligned} &u^{\epsilon,N}\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon,N}+v^{\epsilon,N}\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon,N}-\partial_{yy}u^{\epsilon,N}+u^{\epsilon,N}-u_{0}(N)-\epsilon=0,\\
&\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon,N}+\partial_{y}v^{\epsilon,N}=0,\end{aligned}\right.

(3.61)

with the boundary conditions

u^{\epsilon,N}(x,y)|_{y=0}=\epsilon,\ u^{\epsilon,N}(x,y)|_{y=N}=u_{0}(N)+\epsilon,\ v^{\epsilon,N}(x,y)|_{y=0}=v^{\epsilon,N}(x,y)|_{y=N}=0,\quad x\in(0,L),

(3.62)

and the initial data

u^{\epsilon,N}(x,y)|_{x=0}:={u}_{0}(y)+\epsilon,\quad y\in[0,N].

(3.63)

Applying the classical von-Mises transformation
(i.e.,
\eta:=\int_{0}^{y}u^{\epsilon,N}(x,y^{\prime})dy^{\prime}
), the system (
3.61
)
can be translated into a quasi-linear parabolic equation that will not cause
the loss of
x-
derivative.
Then, it is easy to obtain the local-in-time well-posedness with
higher order Sobolev regularity for this translated system.
Due to the non-degeneracy boundary of horizontal velocity in (
3.62
),
the variable
\eta
after von-Mises transformation
is equivalent to the vertical variable
y
in the original Eulerian coordinates.
Then, the system (
3.61
)-(
3.63
) be obtained local
well-posedness with higher Sobolev regularity on the life span
[0,L^{\epsilon}]
.
For more details on the results of this local existence, the reader can refer to
the Lemma 2.2 in
[
20
]
.
Then, applying the a priori estimates given in Proposition
3.1

to the solution
({u}^{\epsilon,N},{v}^{\epsilon,N})
, we obtain a uniform life span time

L_{a}>0
and a constant
C_{1}
(independent of
\epsilon
and
N
) such that it holds true

\sum_{0\leq 2\alpha+\beta\leq 2k+1}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{\beta}\partial_{y}{v}^{\epsilon,N}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}[0,N]}\leq C_{1},

(3.64)

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}y\leq{u}^{\epsilon,N}(x,y)\leq 2(y+\epsilon),\ (x,y)\in[0,L_{a}]\times[0,\delta_{0}],

(3.65)

\displaystyle{\displaystyle u}^{\epsilon,N}(x,y)\geq\frac{1}{4}\delta_{0},\ (x,y)\in[0,L_{a}]\times[\delta_{0},N].

Based on the uniform estimates for
({u}^{\epsilon,N},{v}^{\epsilon,N})
,
one can pass the limit
\epsilon\rightarrow 0^{+}
and
N\rightarrow+\infty

to get a solution
(u,v)
satisfying (
1.6
) by using a strong compactness arguments.
Indeed, it follows from estimate that
\partial_{y}{v}^{\epsilon,N}
is bounded uniformly in

L^{\infty}([0,L_{a}];H^{2k+1}_{l}([0,N]))
,
and
\partial_{x}(\partial_{y}v^{\epsilon,N}([0,N]))
is bounded
uniformly in
L^{2}([0,L_{a}];H^{2k-1}_{l}([0,N]))
.
Then, it follows from a strong compactness argument,
after taking a subsequence
\epsilon_{k}\rightarrow 0^{+}
,

\partial_{y}{v}^{\epsilon_{k},N}\ {\rightharpoonup}\ w^{N}\quad{\rm in}~L^{2}([0,L_{a}];H^{2k+1}_{l}([0,N])),

and

\partial_{y}{v}^{\epsilon_{k},N}\ {\longrightarrow}\ w^{N}\quad{\rm in}~C([0,L_{a}];H^{2k-2}([0,N])),

where
w^{N}\in L^{2}([0,L_{a}];H^{2k+1}_{l}([0,N]))\cap L^{\infty}([0,L_{a}];H^{2k-2}([0,N]))
.
Let us define
v^{N}(x,y):=\int_{0}^{y}w^{N}(x,y^{\prime})dy^{\prime}
and

u^{N}(x,y):={u}_{0}(y)-\int_{0}^{x}w^{N}(x^{\prime},y)dx^{\prime}
, it holds true

\partial_{x}u^{N}(x,y)+\partial_{y}v^{N}(x,y)=0.

Furthermore, it is easy to deduce that

\displaystyle{u}^{\epsilon_{k},N}(x,y)-u^{N}(x,y)

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{x}\partial_{y}{v}^{\epsilon_{k},N}(x^{\prime},y)dx^{\prime}+{u}_{0}(y)+\epsilon_{k}-{u}_{0}(y)+\int_{0}^{x}w(x^{\prime},y)dx^{\prime}

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{x}(\partial_{y}{v}^{\epsilon_{k},N}(x^{\prime},y)-w^{N}(x^{\prime},y))dx^{\prime}+\epsilon_{k},

and hence, we have

\displaystyle\|{u}^{\epsilon_{k},N}-u^{N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}H^{2k-2}([0,N])}

\displaystyle\leq\|\int_{0}^{x}(\partial_{y}{v}^{\epsilon_{k},N}(x^{\prime},y)-w^{N}(x^{\prime},y))dx^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}H^{2k-2}([0,N])}+\epsilon_{k}\sqrt{N}

\displaystyle\leq L_{a}\|\partial_{y}{v}^{\epsilon_{k},N}-w^{N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}H^{2k-2}([0,N])}+\epsilon_{k}\sqrt{N}.

Therefore, it holds true

{u}^{\epsilon_{k},N}\ {\longrightarrow}\ u^{N}\quad{\rm in}~L^{\infty}([0,L_{a}];H^{2k-2}([0,N])).

Therefore, the solution
(u^{N}(x,y),v^{N}(x,y))
satisfies

\left\{\begin{aligned} &u^{N}\partial_{x}u^{N}+v^{N}\partial_{y}u^{N}-\partial_{yy}u^{N}+u^{N}-u_{0}(N)=0,\ (x,y)\in[0,L_{a}]\times(0,N);\\
&\partial_{x}u^{N}+\partial_{y}v^{N}=0,\ (x,y)\in[0,L_{a}]\in(0,N),\end{aligned}\right.

with the boundary condition

u^{N}(x,y)|_{y=0}=0,\quad u^{N}(x,y)|_{y=N}=u_{0}(N),\quad v^{N}(x,y)|_{y=0}=0,\quad x\in(0,L_{a}),

and the initial data

u^{N}(x,y)|_{x=0}={u}_{0}(y),\quad y\in[0,N].

Furthermore, the solution
(u^{N},v^{N})
also satisfies the estimates

\sum_{0\leq 2\alpha+\beta\leq 2k+1}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\partial_{y}^{\beta}\partial_{y}v^{N}\langle y\rangle^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}[0,N]}\leq C_{k,l,\delta_{0}}(1+C(u_{0})),

(3.66)

and

\frac{1}{4}y\leq u^{N}(x,y)\leq 2y,\ (x,y)\in[0,L_{a}]\times[0,\delta_{0}];\quad\quad u^{N}(x,y)\geq\frac{1}{4}\delta_{0},\ (x,y)\in[0,L_{a}]\times[\delta_{0},N].

(3.67)

Since the estimate (
3.66
) is bounded independent of
N
,
and similarly taking
N\rightarrow+\infty
, it is easy to obtain the solution

(u,v)
to the original problem Eqs.(
1.6
)-(
1.7
).
At the same time, the estimates (
3.66
) and (
3.67
) hold on
over the domain
[0,+\infty)
. Therefore, we complete the proof of local
existence and estimate (
1.13
) in Theorem
1.1
.

3.3.  Uniqueness of Prandtl-Hartmann system

In this subsection, we will show the uniqueness of solution to the original
system (
1.6
).
Let
(u_{1},v_{1})
and
(u_{2},v_{2})
be two solutions in the existence time
[0,L_{a}]

constructed in the previous subsection, with respect to the same initial data.
Let us set

(\widehat{u},\widehat{v},\widehat{q}):=(u_{2}-u_{1},v_{2}-v_{1},\frac{v_{2}-v_{1}}{u_{2}})

then they satisfy the following evolution

(u_{2})^{2}\partial_{y}\widehat{q}+\partial_{y}^{2}\widehat{u}-\widehat{u}=\widehat{u}\partial_{x}u_{1}+v_{1}\partial_{y}\widehat{u}.

Taking
\partial_{x}
operator to the above equation and
using the divergence-free condition, we have

\partial_{x}\{(u_{2})^{2}\partial_{y}\widehat{q}\}-\partial_{y}^{3}\widehat{v}+\partial_{y}\widehat{v}=\partial_{x}(\widehat{u}\partial_{x}u_{1}+v_{1}\partial_{y}\widehat{u}),

(3.68)

with the zero boundary condition and initial data

(\widehat{u},\widehat{v})|_{y=0}=0,\quad\lim_{y\rightarrow+\infty}\widehat{u}=0,\quad\widehat{u}|_{x=0}=0.

(3.69)

Next, we will establish the following estimate.

Proposition 3.4
.

Let
(u_{1},v_{1})
and
(u_{2},v_{2})
be two solutions of the equation (
1.6
)
with the same initial data (
1.2
), and satisfying the estimate
(
1.13
) respectively.
Then, the quantity
(\widehat{u},\widehat{v},\widehat{q})
given by satisfies

\|(u_{2}\partial_{y}\widehat{q})(x)\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{x}\|(\sqrt{u_{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}\widehat{q})(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}d\tau+\int_{0}^{x}\|\sqrt{u_{2}}\partial_{y}\widehat{q}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}d\tau\leq C\int_{0}^{x}\|(u_{2}\partial_{y}\widehat{q})(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{y}}^{2}d\tau,

(3.70)

for any
x\in(0,L_{a}]
.

Proof of Uniqueness.
.

Applying the Gronwall’s lemma to the estimate (
3.70
),
we obtain
(u_{2}\partial_{y}\widehat{q})(x,y)\equiv 0
.
Due to the fact
u_{2}>0
in the fluid domain, then we have

v_{2}-v_{1}=u_{2}w

for some function
w:=w(x)
.
Using the assumption
u_{2}>0
in the fluid domain
and boundary condition
\widehat{v}|_{y=0}=0
,
we know that
w\equiv 0
and hence
v_{2}\equiv v_{1}
.
Using the divergence-free condition, it holds

u_{2}(x,y)-u_{1}(x,y)=\int_{0}^{x}\partial_{\tau}\{u_{2}(\tau,y)-u_{1}(\tau,y)\}d\tau=-\int_{0}^{x}\partial_{y}\{v_{2}(\tau,y)-v_{1}(\tau,y)\}d\tau=0.

Then, we have
u_{2}(x,y)\equiv u_{1}(x,y)
.
This proves the uniqueness of solution in Theorem
1.1
.
∎

In the rest of this subsection, we will give the proof
of Proposition
3.4
as follows.

Proof of Proposition
3.4
.

Multiplying the equation (
3.68
)
by
\partial_{y}\widehat{q}
integrating over
[0,x]\times[0,+\infty)

and integrating by part, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\!(u_{2}^{2}|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}|^{2})(x,y)dy+\!\int_{0}^{x}\!\!\int_{0}^{\infty}\!\!u_{2}|\partial_{y}^{2}\widehat{q}|^{2}dyd\tau+\!\int_{0}^{x}\!\!\int_{0}^{\infty}\!\!u_{2}|\partial_{y}^{2}\widehat{q}|^{2}dyd\tau+\!\int_{0}^{x}\!\!2\partial_{y}u_{2}|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}|^{2}|_{y=0}d\tau

(3.71)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{\infty}u_{2}\partial_{x}u_{2}|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}|^{2}dyd\tau-\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{\infty}(2\partial_{y}u_{2}\partial_{y}\widehat{q}+\partial_{y}^{2}u_{2}\widehat{q})\cdot\partial_{y}^{2}\widehat{q}dyd\tau

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{y}u_{2}\widehat{q}\partial_{y}\widehat{q}dyd\tau+\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{\infty}(\partial_{x}v_{1}\partial_{y}\widehat{u}-v_{1}\partial_{y}^{2}\widehat{v}+\partial_{y}\widehat{u}\partial_{y}v_{1}-\widehat{u}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}v_{1})\partial_{y}\widehat{q}dyd\tau

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle III_{1}+III_{2}+III_{3}+III_{4}+III_{5}.

Similar to the estimates in Lemma
3.3
, we can obtain

\displaystyle|III_{1}|

\displaystyle\leq(1+\|\partial_{y}u_{2}\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}v_{2}\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}\partial_{x}^{\langle 1\rangle}v_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{\infty}|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}|^{2}dyd\tau,

\displaystyle|III_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{x}\partial_{y}u_{2}|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}|^{2}|_{y=0}dx+C(\|\partial_{y}^{2}u_{2}\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}v_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{\infty}|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}|^{2}dyd\tau,

\displaystyle|III_{3}|

\displaystyle\leq(\|\partial_{y}^{3}u_{2}\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}^{4}v_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{\infty}|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}|^{2}d\tau dy,

\displaystyle|III_{4}|

\displaystyle\leq(\|\partial_{y}u_{2}\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}v_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\int_{0}^{x}\int_{0}^{\infty}|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}|^{2}dyd\tau.

It is easy to check that

\displaystyle|III_{5}|\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}v_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}\widehat{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}+\|v_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}^{2}\widehat{v}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{x}\widehat{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}v_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}+\|\widehat{u}\langle y\rangle^{-1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{y}}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{y}v_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\|\partial_{y}v_{1}\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{x}\partial_{y}v_{1}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}v_{1}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\|\partial_{y}^{2}\widehat{v}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}.

Recall the definition
\widehat{q}:=\frac{v_{2}-v_{1}}{u_{2}}=\frac{\widehat{v}}{u_{2}}
,
then it holds true

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}^{2}\widehat{v}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\leq

\displaystyle(\|\partial_{y}u_{2}\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}u_{2}\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}v_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}^{3}v_{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle+(1+\|\partial_{y}u_{2}\langle y\rangle|_{x=0}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}\langle y\rangle\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}})\|\sqrt{u_{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}\widehat{q}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}.

Thus, collecting the estimates from
III_{1}
to
III_{5}
, we can get

|III_{1}+III_{2}+III_{3}+III_{4}+III_{5}|\leq\int_{0}^{x}\partial_{y}u_{2}|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}|^{2}|_{y=0}dx+\frac{1}{4}\|\sqrt{u_{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}\widehat{q}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+C\|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}^{2}.

(3.72)

Similar to the estimate (
A.4
), it is easy to obtain

\|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}\leq 4C\|u_{2}\partial_{y}\widehat{q}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}+\frac{1}{4C}\|\sqrt{u_{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}\widehat{q}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}},

which, together with estimate (
3.72
), yields directly

|III_{1}+III_{2}+III_{3}+III_{4}+III_{5}|\leq\int_{0}^{x}\partial_{y}u_{2}|\partial_{y}\widehat{q}|^{2}|_{y=0}dx+\frac{1}{2}\|\sqrt{u_{2}}\partial_{y}^{2}\widehat{q}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}^{2}+4C^{2}\|u_{2}\partial_{y}\widehat{q}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{y}}.

(3.73)

Substituting the estimate (
3.73
) into the inequality (
3.71
),
then we obtain the claim estimate (
3.70
).
Therefore, we complete the proof of Proposition
3.4
.
∎

4
Asymptotic behavior of Prandtl-Hartmann system

In this section, we aim to investigate the asymptotic behaviour of velocity profiles in the 2-D magnetic Prandtl boundary layer.
More specifically, we would like to establish the decay estimate in
x
variable, thus
C
denotes a generic positive constant independent of
x
which may vary in following different estimates in this section.

Then an important question is whether the system (
1.6
) is globally well-posedness in Sobolev space when the initial data are near the Hartmann layer.
More precisely, we study the global stability of the Hartmann layer
(\bar{u},\bar{v})=(1-e^{-y},0)
.
Thus, we need to define an appropriate notion of the difference between two pairs
(u,v)
and
(\bar{u},\bar{v})
. The notion we used in our case does not coincide with pointwise perturbations
u(x,y)-\bar{u}(y)
and
v(x,y)
, but rather the modulated perturbation
\phi(x,\psi):=u^{2}(x,\psi)-\bar{u}^{2}(\psi)
.
Then it follows from (
1.9
) and (
1.10
) that the difference
\phi
satisfies

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\phi_{x}-u\phi_{\psi\psi}+2\frac{\phi}{\bar{u}(u+\bar{u})}=0,\quad(x,\psi)\in\mathbb{R}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}_{+},\\
\phi|_{x=0}=\phi_{0}(\psi):=u_{0}^{2}(\psi)-\bar{u}_{0}^{2}(\psi),\quad\psi\in\mathbb{R}_{+},\\
\phi|_{\psi=0}=0,~~\phi|_{\psi\rightarrow\infty}=0,\quad x\in\mathbb{R}_{+}.\end{array}\right.

(4.1)

We rewrite
\eqref{eq3}_{1}
as

\phi_{x}+L\phi=0,

where the operator
L\phi:=-u\phi_{\psi\psi}+2\frac{\phi}{\bar{u}(u+\bar{u})}
.
The global existence and the asymptotic behaviour of the solution
(u,v)(x,y)
to (
1.6
) can be easily translated into that of the perturbed solution
\phi(x,\psi)
.
And it is universally known that the global existence of solutions for system (
4.1
) will be obtained by combining the local existence result with some a priori estimates and then employing the standard continuity argument.
Thus, we first review the classical local existence result, which can be obtained by a similar way to Oleinik’s result in
[
42
]
. And we omit detalis of proof here.

Proposition 4.1
.

Assume that
u_{0}(y)>0
for
y>0
;
u_{0}(0)=0
,
u_{0}^{\prime}(0)>0
,
u_{0}(y)\rightarrow 1
as
y\rightarrow\infty
;
u_{0}(y)
,
u^{\prime}_{0}(y)
,
u^{\prime\prime}_{0}(y)
are bounded for
0\leq y<\infty
and satisfy the Hölder condition. Moreover, assume that for small
y
the following compatiblity condition is satisfied at the point
(0,0)
:

-u^{\prime\prime}_{0}(y)+u_{0}(y)-1=O(y^{2}).

Then, for some
X>0
there exists a solution
(u,v)
of problem (
1.6
) and (
1.7
) in
D:=\{0<x<X,0<y<\infty\}
, which has the following properties:
u(x,y)
is bounded and continuous in
\bar{D}
,
u>0
for
y>0
;
u_{y}>m>0
for
0<y\leq y_{0}
, where
m
and
y_{0}
are constants;
u_{y}
and
u_{yy}
are bounded and continuous in
D
;
u_{x}
,
v
and
v_{y}
are bounded and continuous in any finite portion of
\bar{D}
.

It is easy to check that the local existence above is also valid for system (
4.1
) in new variables
(x,\psi)
.
First, we set
I:=[0,x]
, and denote the norms

\displaystyle f(x):=

\displaystyle\underset{x^{\prime}\in I}{\sup}\Big\{\|\frac{u}{\bar{u}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}},\|\frac{\bar{u}}{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\Big\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}(x):=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0,1}\sup_{x^{\prime}\in I}\Big(\|\partial_{x}^{k}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\partial_{x}^{k}\phi}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{\psi}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\partial_{x}^{k}\phi}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\Big),

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}(x):=

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}(x)+\sum_{k=0,1}\left\{\Big\|\|\sqrt{u}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{\psi}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\Big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\Big\|\|\frac{\partial_{x}^{k}\phi}{u}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\Big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0,1}\left\{\Big\|\|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{\psi}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\Big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\Big\|\|\frac{\partial_{x}^{k}\phi}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\Big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\Big\|\|\frac{\partial_{x}^{k+1}\phi}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\Big\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\right\}.

Based on the local existence result, we can obtain the global existence and asympotic stability results as follow.

Proposition 4.2

(Global well-posedness and asymptotic stability in von-Mises variable)
.

Under the conditions of Proposition
4.1
, assume that
initial data satisfies
f(0)\leq\gamma_{0}
with any positive constant
\gamma_{0}>1
.
Furthermore, there exists a small positive constanst
\sigma_{0}
such that

\|\phi_{0\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{0}}{u_{0}^{3/2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|(L\phi_{0})_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{L\phi_{0}}{u_{0}^{3/2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq\sigma_{0},

(4.2)

then the system (
4.1
) has a global-in-
x
solution

\phi
satisfying for any
0<x<\infty
,

f(x)\leq 2\gamma_{0},\quad\mathcal{D}(x)\leq C_{1}\mathcal{E}(0),

and the decay estimate

\mathcal{E}(x)\leq C_{2}e^{-x}.

(4.3)

Here
C_{1}
and
C_{2}
are positive constants independent of
x
.

Remark 4.1
.

The initial condition
f(0)\leq\gamma_{0}
implies that
u
and
\bar{u}

tend to zero in the same order near the boundary
\{\psi=0\}
.
And in general
u\neq\bar{u}
, thus it is reasonable to assume that
\gamma_{0}>1
.

Remark 4.2
.

Indeed, we require the initial data satisfy the compatibility conditions:

(\phi_{x}-L\phi)|_{x=0}=(\phi_{x\psi}-L(\phi_{\psi}))|_{x=0}=0
in Proposition
4.2
.
At the same time, we apply the fact
u\lesssim 1
to check that

\mathcal{E}(0)\leq\|\phi_{0\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{0}}{u_{0}^{3/2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|(L\phi_{0})_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{L\phi_{0}}{u_{0}^{3/2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}.

This is reason why we require the small initial data (
4.2
)
instead of
\mathcal{E}(0)\leq\sigma_{0}
.

4.1.  A priori estimates

The global existence of solution will be obtained by combing the local existence
result with a priori estimates.
Since the local existence has been built in Proposition
4.1
,
our main task is to establish the closed a priori estimate.
Therefore, for any small constant
\sigma>0
and positive constant
\gamma>1
,
we assume a priori estimates

f(x)\leq 2\gamma,\quad\mathcal{E}(x)\leq\sigma.

(4.4)

Thus, we will establish some estimates for the system (
4.1
)
under the a priori estimates (
4.4
).
Let us denote

\omega:=\frac{\phi}{u+\bar{u}}=u-\bar{u}.

Next, we analyze the properties of the solution
\bar{u}
for classical Hartmann boundary layer. We claim that there exists a positive constant
\eta_{0}
such that

\displaystyle\bar{u}\sim\sqrt{\psi}~~\text{for}~~0<\psi\leq\eta_{0}~~\text{and}~~\bar{u}\gtrsim 1~~\text{for}~~\psi\geq\eta_{0},

(4.5)

where we recall again that
A\lesssim B
(
A\gtrsim B
) represents that there exists a positive constant
C
such that
A\leq CB
(
A\geq CB
),
and
A\sim B
stands for
A\lesssim CB
and
A\gtrsim CB
(see the end of Section
1
). The parameter
\eta_{0}
appearing in (
4.5
) will be chosen in the following proof, which is related to the Taylor expansion of
\bar{u}(y)
.

Indeed, we suppress the dependence of the solution
\bar{u}
on
x
in the following calculations for simplicity.
Using Taylor expansion,
\bar{u}|_{y=0}=0
and
\bar{u}_{y}|_{y=0}=1>0
, we find

\bar{u}(y)=y+o(y).

Then, there exists a positive constant
\eta_{1}
such that

\frac{1}{2}y\leq\bar{u}(y)\leq\frac{3}{2}y,~~\text{for}~~0<y\leq\eta_{1}.

(4.6)

Since
\psi=\int_{0}^{y}\bar{u}(y^{\prime})dy^{\prime}
, then a direct calculation yields for
0<y\leq\eta_{1}
,

\displaystyle\psi\leq\frac{3}{2}\int_{0}^{y}y^{\prime}dy^{\prime}=\frac{3}{4}y^{2},~~~\psi\geq\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{y}y^{\prime}dy^{\prime}=\frac{1}{4}y^{2}.

(4.7)

The estimates (
4.7
), together with another equivalent relation (
4.6
), yields that there exists a positive constant
\eta_{0}=\frac{1}{4}\eta_{1}^{2}
such that

\bar{u}(\psi)\sim\sqrt{\psi},~~\text{for}~~0<\psi\leq\eta_{0}.

The fact
\psi\geq\eta_{0}
implies that there exists a
positive constant
\eta_{2}
such that
y\geq\eta_{2}
. And it is easy to check that

\bar{u}(y)=1-e^{-y}\geq 1-e^{-\eta_{2}}\gtrsim 1,~~\text{for}~~y\geq\eta_{2}.

which immediately implies that

\bar{u}(\psi)\gtrsim 1,~~\text{for}~~\psi\geq\eta_{0}.

This completes the proof of the claim (
4.5
).

First, our aim is to show that
f(x)\leq\gamma
. And the proofs of following estimates in Lemmas
4.3
-
4.4
are inter-connected because of their nonlinear nature, thus it will be convenient for us to give the following notation:

\alpha(x):=\sup_{x^{\prime}\in I}\|\frac{\omega}{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}.

Lemma 4.3
.

Under the assumption (
4.4
), we have

\alpha(x)\leq{\bar{C}f^{\frac{17}{2}}(x)}\mathcal{E}(x),

(4.8)

where
\bar{C}
is a positive constant independent of
x
.

Proof.

It follows from equation
\eqref{eq3}_{1}
that

\|u^{\frac{3}{2}}\phi_{\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq C\big(\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\|\frac{u}{u+\bar{u}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\|\frac{u}{\bar{u}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\big)\leq Cf(x)\big(\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big),

(4.9)

and

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq C\big(\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\|\frac{u}{u+\bar{u}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\|\frac{u}{\bar{u}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\big)\leq Cf(x)\big(\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big).

(4.10)

It is easy to check from the definition of
\mathcal{E}(x)
that the second term on the right handside of (
4.9
) can be controlled by
\mathcal{E}(x)
. For the first term, consider the case where
\psi\geq\eta_{0}
,

\|\frac{\phi(x,\psi)}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}(x,\psi)}(1-\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}}))\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq C\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}},

where the definition of the cutoff function
\chi
can be found in the end of Section
1
.
And for the other case where
0\leq\psi\leq\eta_{0}
, using equivalent relation
\bar{u}\sim\sqrt{\psi}
and Hardy inequality, we get

\displaystyle\|\frac{\phi(x,\psi)}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}(x,\psi)}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq

\displaystyle Cf^{\frac{3}{2}}(x)\|\frac{\phi(x,\psi)}{\psi^{\frac{3}{4}}}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq Cf^{\frac{3}{2}}(x)\big(\|\phi(x,\psi)\psi^{\frac{1}{4}}\chi^{\prime}(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{\psi}(x,\psi)\psi^{\frac{1}{4}}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Cf^{2}(x)\big(\|\sqrt{u}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big),

where the positive constant
C
may depend on
\eta_{0}
.
Combining two estimates above, and using the fact that
f(x)\geq 1
, we find that

\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq Cf^{2}(x)\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big).

(4.11)

Inserting (
4.11
) into (
4.9
), we then obtain that

\|u^{\frac{3}{2}}\phi_{\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq Cf^{3}(x)\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big),

(4.12)

Now returning to estimate (
4.10
), it is easy to find the second term on the right handside of (
4.10
) can be controlled by
\mathcal{E}(x)
.
To obtain the estimate of the first term, we calculate the far-field contribution immediately by using the fact that
u\gtrsim 1
in the support of
1-\chi
:

\displaystyle\|\frac{\phi(x,\psi)}{u^{\frac{5}{2}}(x,\psi)}(1-\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}}))\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}.

And then, we can localize based on the location of
\psi
and use Hardy inequality to find

\displaystyle\|\frac{\phi(x,\psi)}{u^{\frac{5}{2}}(x,\psi)}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq

\displaystyle Cf^{\frac{5}{2}}(x)\|\frac{\phi(x,\psi)}{\psi^{\frac{5}{4}}}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq Cf^{\frac{5}{2}}(x)\big(\|\frac{\phi(x,\psi)}{\psi^{\frac{1}{4}}}\chi^{\prime}(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{\psi}(x,\psi)}{\psi^{\frac{1}{4}}}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Cf^{\frac{5}{2}}(x)\big(\|\phi(x,\psi)\psi^{\frac{3}{4}}\chi^{\prime\prime}(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{\psi}(x,\psi)\psi^{\frac{3}{4}}\chi^{\prime}(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{\psi\psi}(x,\psi)\psi^{\frac{3}{4}}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Cf^{4}(x)\big(\|u^{\frac{3}{2}}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|u^{\frac{3}{2}}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|u^{\frac{3}{2}}\phi_{\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Cf^{7}(x)\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big),

where, we have used the estimate (
4.12
) and the fact that
u\lesssim 1
in the last inequality.
Hence, we obtain that

\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq Cf^{7}(x)\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big).

(4.13)

Upon inserting into (
4.10
) yields that

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq Cf^{8}(x)\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big).

(4.14)

And it then follows from (
4.12
) that

\displaystyle\|\frac{\phi_{\psi}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq

\displaystyle Cf^{\frac{1}{2}}(x)\|\frac{\phi_{\psi}(x,\psi)}{\psi^{\frac{1}{4}}}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+C\|\frac{\phi_{\psi}(x,\psi)}{\sqrt{u(x,\psi)}}(1-\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}}))\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}

(4.15)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Cf^{\frac{1}{2}}(x)\big(\|\phi_{\psi}(x,\psi)\psi^{\frac{3}{4}}\chi^{\prime}(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{\psi\psi}(x,\psi)\psi^{\frac{3}{4}}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)+C\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Cf^{2}(x)\big(\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|u^{\frac{3}{2}}\phi_{\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Cf^{5}(x)\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big).

By Sobolev inequality, we deduce from the estimates (
4.14
)-(
4.15
) that

\displaystyle|\phi_{\psi}|^{2}\leq C\|\frac{\phi_{\psi}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq Cf^{13}(x)\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big),

from which, we obtain

\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq Cf^{\frac{13}{2}}(x)\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big).

(4.16)

We then deduce from (
4.16
) that

\displaystyle\frac{\phi(x,\psi)}{u^{2}(x,\psi)}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\leq\frac{Cf^{2}(x)}{\psi}\int_{0}^{\psi}|\phi_{\psi^{\prime}}|d\psi^{\prime}\leq Cf^{2}(x)\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq Cf^{\frac{17}{2}}(x)\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big).

(4.17)

The far-field term can be estimated easily as follows:

\|\frac{\phi(x,\psi)}{u^{2}(x,\psi)}(1-\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}}))\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq C\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

(4.18)

which, together with (
4.17
), yields that

\displaystyle\|\frac{\phi}{u^{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq Cf^{\frac{17}{2}}(x)\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big).

(4.19)

Recalling the definition of
\omega
, we deduce from (
4.19
) that

\displaystyle\|\frac{\omega}{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}=

\displaystyle\|\frac{\phi}{u(u+\bar{u})}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}=\|\frac{\phi}{u^{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\|\frac{u}{u+\bar{u}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq\|\frac{\phi}{u^{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\displaystyle Cf^{\frac{17}{2}}(x)}\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)\leq{\bar{C}f^{\frac{17}{2}}(x)}\mathcal{E}(x),

which, together with the definition of
\alpha(x)
, gives (
4.8
) immediately.
∎

Lemma 4.4
.

Under the assumption (
4.4
), we have

f(x)\leq\gamma,

(4.20)

provided that
\sigma\leq\min\Big\{\frac{1}{2^{\frac{21}{2}}{\gamma}^{\frac{17}{2}}\bar{C}},\frac{\gamma-1}{2^{\frac{19}{2}}{\gamma}^{\frac{19}{2}}\bar{C}}\Big\}
.

Proof.

The estimate (
4.8
) and the assumption (
4.4
) lead us to obatin

\alpha(x)\leq\bar{C}f^{\frac{17}{2}}(x)\mathcal{E}(x)\leq 2^{\frac{21}{2}}{\gamma}^{\frac{17}{2}}\bar{C}\sigma\leq\frac{1}{4},

(4.21)

provided that
\sigma\leq\frac{1}{2^{\frac{21}{2}}{\gamma}^{\frac{17}{2}}\bar{C}}
.
On the one hand, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle\|\frac{u}{\bar{u}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}=\|\frac{\bar{u}+\omega}{\bar{u}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq 1+\|\frac{\omega}{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\|\frac{u}{\bar{u}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq 1+\alpha(x)\|\frac{u}{\bar{u}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}},

which implies that

\|\frac{u}{\bar{u}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq\frac{1}{1-\alpha(x)}.

(4.22)

On the other hand, we find

\|\frac{\bar{u}}{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq\|\frac{\bar{u}}{\bar{u}+\omega}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq\|\frac{\bar{u}}{\bar{u}-|\omega|}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq\|\frac{1}{1-|\frac{\omega}{\bar{u}}|}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq\|\frac{1}{1-|\frac{\omega}{u}||\frac{u}{\bar{u}}|}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq\frac{1-\alpha(x)}{1-2\alpha(x)}.

(4.23)

Then, recalling the definition of
f(x)
and combining the estimates (
4.21
)-(
4.23
), we have

f(x)\leq\frac{1-\alpha(x)}{1-2\alpha(x)}\leq\frac{1}{1-2\alpha(x)}\leq\frac{1}{1-2^{\frac{19}{2}}{\gamma}^{\frac{17}{2}}\bar{C}\sigma}.

This immediately implies that

f(x)\leq\gamma,

provided that

\sigma\leq\frac{\gamma-1}{2^{\frac{19}{2}}{\gamma}^{\frac{19}{2}}\bar{C}}.

Thus, we have completed the proof of this lemma.
∎

From Lemmas
4.3
-
4.4
, we may deduce that

Corollary 4.5
.

Under the assumption (
4.4
), we have

\alpha(x)\leq C\mathcal{E}(x),

(4.24)

where
C
is a positive constant independent of
x
.

From now on, with (
4.20
), (
4.24
) and our bootstrap assumption (
4.4
) at hand, we will use the fact that
f(x)\lesssim 1
and
\alpha(x)\lesssim 1
in forthcoming estimates.
Next, we will devote ourselves to showing standard energy estimate, quotient estimate and other estimate in
L^{2}
level, that is Lemmas
4.6
-
4.8
. The first one is
L^{2}
standard energy estimate.

Lemma 4.6
.

Under the assumption (
4.4
), we have

\displaystyle\sup_{x^{\prime}\in I}\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\int_{I}\big(\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi}{u}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\big)dx\leq\|\phi_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}.

(4.25)

Moreover, we have the following exponential decay estimate:

\sup_{x^{\prime}\in I}e^{x^{\prime}}\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\int_{I}e^{x^{\prime}}\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}dx^{\prime}\leq\|\phi_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}.

(4.26)

Proof.

Multiplying
\eqref{eq3}_{1}
by
\phi
and integrating with respect to
\psi
, then we compute that

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\big(\|\sqrt{\bar{u}}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi}{\sqrt{\bar{u}(u+\bar{u})}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\big)-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\bar{u}_{\psi\psi}|\phi|^{2}d\psi\leq\int_{0}^{\infty}\big|\omega\phi\phi_{\psi\psi}\big|d\psi.

(4.27)

The last term on the left handside of (
4.27
) is positive, since

\bar{u}_{\psi\psi}=\bar{u}_{yy}-\bar{u}|\bar{u}_{\psi}|^{2}\leq 0.

Now returning to estimate the term on the right handside of (
4.27
) as follows:

\int_{0}^{\infty}\big|\omega\phi\phi_{\psi\psi}\big|d\psi\leq C\|u\omega\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}

(4.28)

We recall the definition of
\omega
and Sobolev inequality, to find

\displaystyle\|u\omega\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq C\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq C\|\frac{\phi}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(4.29)

Combining the estimates (
4.11
) and (
4.20
), we find that

\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq C\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big).

(4.30)

Next, we note from
(
4.14
)
and (
4.20
) that

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq C\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big).

(4.31)

Inserting the estimates (
4.28
)-(
4.31
) into (
4.27
) yields the estimate

\begin{split}\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\big(\|\sqrt{\bar{u}}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi}{\sqrt{\bar{u}(u+\bar{u})}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\big)\leq&C\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)\big(\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi}{u}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\big)\\
\leq&C\mathcal{E}(x)\big(\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi}{u}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\big).\end{split}

It follows the definition of
f(x)
and (
4.20
) that
u\sim\bar{u}
.
Since
\mathcal{E}(x)\leq\sigma
, by choosing
\sigma
small enough, then we have

\frac{d}{dx}\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\big(\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi}{u}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\big)\leq 0,

which, integrating over
I
, yields (
4.25
) immediately. Since
u\lesssim 1
, the estimate above implies that

\frac{d}{dx}\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\leq 0.

Therefore, using Gronwall inequality, we can obtain the decay estimate (
4.26
).
∎

By using the method in
[
28
]
, we then derive the following quotient estimate.

Lemma 4.7
.

Under the assumption (
4.4
), we have

\displaystyle\sup_{x^{\prime}\in I}\|\frac{\phi}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\int_{I}\Big(\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\Big)dx^{\prime}\leq\|\frac{\phi_{0}}{\sqrt{u_{0}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}.

(4.32)

Moreover, we have the following exponential decay estimate:

\sup_{x^{\prime}\in I}e^{x^{\prime}}\|\frac{\phi}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\int_{I}e^{x^{\prime}}\|\frac{\phi}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}dx^{\prime}\leq\|\frac{\phi_{0}}{\sqrt{u_{0}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}.

(4.33)

Proof.

We multiply
\eqref{eq3}_{1}
by
\frac{\phi}{u}
and integrate with respect to
\psi
, to discover

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}\|\frac{\phi}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\Big(\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi}{\sqrt{u\bar{u}(u+\bar{u})}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\Big)\leq C\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\big|\phi^{2}\omega_{x}\big|}{u^{2}}d\psi\leq C\|u\omega_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}.

(4.34)

Now we write

\omega_{x}=\big(\frac{\phi}{u+\bar{u}}\big)_{x}=\frac{\phi_{x}}{u+\bar{u}}-\frac{\phi\omega_{x}}{(u+\bar{u})^{2}},

(4.35)

which gives the estimate

\displaystyle\|u\omega_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq C\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}+C\|\frac{\phi}{u^{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\|u\omega_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}.

(4.36)

Using Sobolev inequality, we get

\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq C\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(4.37)

Invoking (
4.19
) and (
4.20
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\frac{\phi}{u^{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq C\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)\leq C\mathcal{E}(x).

(4.38)

We substitute the estimates (
4.37
)-(
4.38
) into (
4.36
), to discover

\displaystyle\|u\omega_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq C\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+C\mathcal{E}(x)\|u\omega_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq C\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+C\sigma\|u\omega_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}.

We choose
\sigma
small enough to find

\|u\omega_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq C\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\mathcal{E}(x).

(4.39)

Utilizing (
4.39
) in (
4.34
) and using the fact that
u\sim\bar{u}
, we then obtain

\frac{d}{dx}\|\frac{\phi}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\Big(\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\Big)\leq C\mathcal{E}(x)\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}.

Since
\mathcal{E}(x)\leq\sigma
, by choosing
\sigma
small enough, we can deduce that

\frac{d}{dx}\|\frac{\phi}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\Big(\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\Big)\leq 0,

which implies that (
4.32
). According to the fact that
u\lesssim 1
, we conclude

\frac{d}{dx}\|\frac{\phi}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\leq 0.

Then using Gronwall inequality, we obatin the decay estimate (
4.33
) immediately.
∎

Finally, we also need the following energy estimate.

Lemma 4.8
.

Under the assumption (
4.4
), we have

\displaystyle\sup_{x^{\prime}\in I}\big(\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\big)+\int_{I}\|\frac{\phi_{x^{\prime}}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}dx^{\prime}\leq\mathcal{E}^{2}(0)+C\mathcal{E}(x)\mathcal{D}^{2}(x),

(4.40)

where
C
is a positive constant independent of
x
.

Proof.

Multiplying
\eqref{eq3}_{1}
by
\frac{\phi_{x}}{u}
, using equivalent relation
u\sim\bar{u}
, and integrating by parts, we deduce

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}\big(\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi}{\sqrt{u\bar{u}(u+\bar{u})}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\big)+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\leq C\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{|\phi|^{2}|\omega_{x}|}{u^{4}}d\psi\leq C\|\frac{\omega_{x}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}.

(4.41)

Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{E}(x)
, then combinining (
4.35
) with (
4.38
), we get

\displaystyle\|\omega_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi}{u^{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\|\omega_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}+C\mathcal{E}(x)\|\omega_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}.

Recalling assumption (
4.4
) that
\mathcal{E}(x)\leq\sigma
, choosing
\sigma
small enough, and using Sobolev inequality, we then have

\displaystyle\|\omega_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}^{2}\leq C\big(\|\frac{\phi_{x}(x,\psi)}{u(x,\psi)}(1-\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}}))\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}(x,\psi)}{u(x,\psi)}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}^{2}\big)

(4.42)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\psi^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi_{x\psi}(x,\psi)\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\|\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+C\int_{\psi}^{\infty}(|\phi_{x\psi^{\prime}}(x,\psi^{\prime})|^{2}+\psi^{\prime}\phi_{x\psi^{\prime}\psi^{\prime}}(x,\psi^{\prime})\phi_{x\psi^{\prime}}(x,\psi^{\prime})\chi(\frac{\psi^{\prime}}{\eta_{0}}))d\psi^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\|u^{\frac{3}{2}}\phi_{x\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big),

where we have used that

\begin{split}|\frac{\phi_{x}(x,\psi)}{u(x,\psi)}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})|\leq&\frac{C}{\psi^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\min\{\psi,2\eta_{0}\}}|\phi_{x\psi^{\prime}}(x,\psi^{\prime})|d\psi^{\prime}\leq\frac{C}{\psi^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\psi}|\phi_{x\psi^{\prime}}(x,\psi^{\prime})\chi(\frac{\psi^{\prime}}{\eta_{0}})|d\psi^{\prime}\\
\leq&\frac{C}{\psi^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\psi^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi_{x\psi}(x,\psi)\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\int_{0}^{\psi}\psi^{\prime-\frac{1}{2}}d\psi^{\prime}\leq C\|\psi^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi_{x\psi}(x,\psi)\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}.\end{split}

Next differentiate
\eqref{eq3}_{1}
with respect to
x
, then

\displaystyle\phi_{xx}-u\phi_{x\psi\psi}+2\frac{\phi_{x}}{\bar{u}(u+\bar{u})}-\omega_{x}\phi_{\psi\psi}-2\frac{\phi\omega_{x}}{\bar{u}(u+\bar{u})^{2}}=0.

(4.43)

Combining this equation, (
4.13
), (
4.20
), (
4.31
) with (
4.42
), for any small
\epsilon
(
\epsilon
will be determined later), it holds

\displaystyle\|u^{\frac{3}{2}}\phi_{x\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{xx}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\|\omega_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\|\omega_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{xx}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)

\displaystyle\times\big(\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u^{\frac{3}{2}}\phi_{x\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\epsilon\|u^{\frac{3}{2}}\phi_{x\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+C_{\epsilon}\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{xx}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big),

where we have used the fact that
u\lesssim 1
and

\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq C\mathcal{E}(x)\leq C\sigma.

We then choose
\epsilon
small enough, to find

\|u^{\frac{3}{2}}\phi_{x\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq C\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{xx}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big).

(4.44)

Recalling (
4.35
) and (
4.38
), we deduce

\displaystyle\|\frac{\omega_{x}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq

\displaystyle\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi}{u^{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\|\frac{\omega_{x}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+C\mathcal{E}(x)\|\frac{\omega_{x}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}.

(4.45)

It then follows from a similar way as (
4.13
) that

\displaystyle\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq

\displaystyle\|\frac{\phi_{x}(x,\psi)}{u^{\frac{5}{2}}(x,\psi)}(1-\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}}))\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}(x,\psi)}{\psi^{\frac{5}{4}}}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}

(4.46)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}(x,\psi)}{\psi^{\frac{1}{4}}}\chi^{\prime}(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x\psi}(x,\psi)}{\psi^{\frac{1}{4}}}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x}(x,\psi)\psi^{\frac{3}{4}}\chi^{\prime\prime}(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x\psi}(x,\psi)\psi^{\frac{3}{4}}\chi^{\prime}(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x\psi\psi}(x,\psi)\psi^{\frac{3}{4}}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|u^{\frac{3}{2}}\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|u^{\frac{3}{2}}\phi_{x\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{xx}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big),

where we have used the estimate (
4.44
) in the last inequality.
Therefore, combining (
4.46
) with (
4.45
), and choosing
\sigma
small enough, we discover

\displaystyle\|\frac{\omega_{x}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq C\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{xx}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big).

(4.47)

Using Sobolev inequality, we have

\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(4.48)

We thereupon conclude from (
4.13
), (
4.20
), (
4.47
) and (
4.48
) that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{|\phi|^{2}|\omega_{x}|}{u^{4}}d\psi\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{xx}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)

\displaystyle\times\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big).

Substituting the estimate above into (
4.41
), and then integrating over
I
, by using equivalent relation
u\sim\bar{u}
and recalling the definition of
\mathcal{E}(x)
and
\mathcal{D}(x)
, we obtain the estimate (
4.40
) immediately.
∎

At the
H^{1}
level, comparing equation (
4.1
) versus equation (
4.43
), the nonlinear terms changes and thus requires a new treatment, see Lemmas
4.9
-
4.11
below.
Firstly, we derive the following standard energy estimate in
H^{1}
level.

Lemma 4.9
.

Under the assumption (
4.4
), we have

\sup_{x^{\prime}\in I}\|\phi_{x^{\prime}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\int_{I}\big(\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{x^{\prime}\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi_{x^{\prime}}}{u}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}\big)dx^{\prime}\leq\mathcal{E}^{2}(0)+C\mathcal{E}(x)\mathcal{D}^{2}(x),

(4.49)

where
C
is a positive constant independent of
x
.

Proof.

Multiplying (
4.43
) by
\phi_{x}
and integrating with respect to
\psi
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dx}\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{\bar{u}(u+\bar{u})}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\bar{u}_{\psi\psi}|\phi_{x}|^{2}d\psi

(4.50)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\Big(\big|\omega_{x}\phi_{x}\phi_{\psi\psi}\big|+\frac{\big|\phi\phi_{x}\omega_{x}\big|}{u^{3}}+\big|\omega_{\psi}\phi_{x}\phi_{x\psi}\big|\Big)d\psi.

Since
\bar{u}_{\psi\psi}\leq 0
, we have

\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\bar{u}_{\psi\psi}|\phi_{x}|^{2}d\psi\leq 0.

We now proceed to estimate three terms on the right handside of (
4.50
).
According to (
4.31
) and (
4.39
), we find

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\big|\omega_{x}\phi_{x}\phi_{\psi\psi}\big|d\psi\leq

\displaystyle C\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\|u\omega_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}

(4.51)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{\sqrt{u}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Next, we deduce from (
4.13
), (
4.39
) and (
4.46
) that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\big|\phi\phi_{x}\omega_{x}\big|}{u^{3}}d\psi\leq

\displaystyle C\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\|u\omega_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}

(4.52)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{x}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}

\displaystyle+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{xx}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big)\|\phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\phi_{x\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

In order to estimate the last term on the right handside of (
4.50
), we first control the following term by using a similar way as (
4.17
) and (
4.18
)

\displaystyle\|\frac{\phi}{u^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\leq C\big(\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}+\|\frac{\phi_{\psi}(x,\psi)}{\sqrt{u(x,\psi)}}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}\big).

(4.53)

It then follows from a similar way as (
4.30
) that

\|\frac{\phi_{\psi}}{u^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\leq C\big(\|\phi_{\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}+\|\sqrt{u}\phi_{\psi\psi}\|_{L^{2}_{\psi}}\big).

(4.54)

For the enhanced localized
\frac{\phi_{\psi}}{\sqrt{u}}
estimate, we deduce from (
4.31
) and (
4.54
) that

\displaystyle\|\frac{\phi_{\psi}(x,\psi)}{\sqrt{u(x,\psi)}}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}^{2}\leq\|\frac{\phi_{\psi}(x,\psi)}{\psi^{\frac{1}{4}}}\chi(\frac{\psi}{\eta_{0}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\psi}}^{2}

(4.55)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{\psi}^{\infty}\Big|\frac{\phi_{\psi^{\prime}}(x,\psi^{\prime})}{\psi^{\prime\frac{1}{4}}}\chi(\frac{\psi^{\prime}}{\eta_{0}})\big(\frac{\phi_{\psi^{\prime}}(x,\psi^{\prime})}{\psi^{\prime\frac{1}{4}}}\chi(\frac{\psi^{\prime}}{\eta_{0}})\big)_{\psi^{\prime}}\Big|d{\psi^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=
